# API Surface

_Living artifact. Initial seed by `/andromeda-route` Phase 6 from arch's Standard Contracts. Regenerated by `/wrap-session` Phase 5 reconcile from actual code state after each session ends (per `integrity-protocol.md` Part A + Part B)._

<!-- METADATA start -->
**Last reconciled:** 2026-05-11T18:40:00Z
**Tooling:** `for crate in crates/*; do (cd "$crate" && cargo +nightly public-api --simplified); done` (per-crate; cargo-public-api 0.51 requires nightly + does not support workspace-level invocation)
**Source:** arch.md §Standard Contracts (IPC methods / HTTP endpoints / event topics / schema files / public exports) + §Occupied Resources — seed; actual code via `cargo public-api` per-crate iteration — reconcile
**Maintenance:** wrap-session Phase 5. **2026-05-11 wrap session 54:** chunk #45 (wasmtime Component Model + WIT substrate) surfaces. Public-API delta: +888 lines vs session 53 baseline (5526 vs 4638). Cause: the `plugins` crate's library surface expanded substantially — `engine::build_engine`, `engine::MAX_WASM_HTTP_FIELDS_SIZE_BYTES`, `wit_loader::load_component` + `load_component_for_category` + `linker_for` + `MAX_COMPONENT_BYTES`, `contract::PluginCategory` enum + 3 new `Error` variants (`EngineInit`, `WitLoad`, `ComponentInstantiate`). Note also that the wasmtime 43+ dep adds significant transitive trait surfaces to plugins' downstream consumers (ui-bridge, pulse-app) when they `use plugins::...` — visible in api-surface as expanded "impl" lines per type. 2026-05-11 wrap session 53 + earlier maintenance notes preserved in git history at `.andromeda/context/api-surface.md` prior commits.
<!-- METADATA end -->
